### Hard Audit on the Selected Logic Branches

**Branch:** Sgt. Gray (Greed) at 03:00 AM in Gazebo

#### Step-by-Step Audit:

1. **Disjunction Validation (P or Q):**
   - P: Sgt. Gray was in the Gazebo at 03:00 AM.
   - Q: Mr. Boddy was in the Cloak Room at 03:00 AM.
   - From Clue 3: Mr. Boddy was in the Cloak Room at 03:00 AM if and only if Sgt. Gray was in the Gazebo at 01:00 AM.
   - Sgt. Gray was in the Billiard Room at 01:00 AM, not the Gazebo. Therefore, both P and Q are false.
   - **LOGICAL ERROR: DISJUNCTION VIOLATED.**

2. **Biconditional Audit (P if and only if Q):**
   - P: Sgt. Gray was in the Gazebo at 03:00 AM.
   - Q: The Revolver was in the Gazebo at 03:00 AM.
   - Mechanically:
     - At 01:00 AM: Sgt. Gray was in the Billiard Room.
     - At 02:00 AM: Sgt. Gray moved to the Gazebo.
     - At 03:00 AM: Sgt. Gray was in the Gazebo, and Revolver was not in the Gazebo.
   - Both P and Q are mismatched. Revolver was in the Hall at 10:00 PM, moved to the Gazebo at 03:00 AM, and Sgt. Gray was there at 03:00 AM.
     - **LOGICAL ERROR DETECTED: BRANCH IS CONTRADICTION.**

3. **Contradiction Search:**
   - Review of all clues indicates that Mr. Boddy was in the Billiard Room at 01:00 AM.
   - At 01:00 AM:
     - Sgt. Gray was in the Billiard Room.
     - Revolver was still in the Hall.
   - No contradiction is found within this branch.

4. **Manhattan Falsification:**
   - Move from Hall at 10:00 PM to Gazebo at 03:00 AM:
     - Math: |1-1| + |0-1| = 1
   - Move from Billiard Room at 01:00 AM to Gazebo at 03:00 AM:
     - Math: |0-1| + |0-1| = 2
   - The second move is greater than 1, hence:
     - **PHYSICAL VIOLATION DETECTED.**

5. **Alone Test:**
   - At 03:00 AM, only Sgt. Gray and Mr. Boddy are in the Gazebo.
   - No other suspect was alone in the Gazebo at this time.

### Step 1: Organize the Clues

**Names of Suspects:**
- Mr. Green
- Monsieur Brunette
- Mrs. White
- Sgt. Gray
- Mrs. Peacock

**Weapons:**
- Revolver
- Poison
- Wrench

**Possible Motives:**
- Revenge
- Pride
- Anger
- Greed
- Hatred

- The murderer used the Revolver.

**Murder Room:**
- One of the following: Billiard Room, Hall, Trophy Room, Cloak Room, Gazebo, Drawing Room.

**Murder Times:**
- 10:00 PM, 11:00 PM, 12:00 AM, 01:00 AM, 02:00 AM, 03:00 AM

### Step 2: Initial Clue Analysis

Let's break down the clues and start placing suspects at the beginning:

**Initial Setup:**
- Mr. Boddy (dead or alive). For simplification, assume he is not present at 10:00 PM.
- We need to track the movement of each suspect based on the provided clues.

**Clue 1:**
- The suspect motivated by Hatred was in the same room as Mr. Boddy at 12:00 AM.
  - Follow up: Assume Hatred is Mr. Green, Mrs. White, Mrs. Peacock, or Sgt. Gray. We will test each.
  
**Clue 2:**
- Mr. Green was in the Cloak Room at 11:00 PM.

**Clue 3 (Branch 1 & Branch 2):**
- Mr. Green or Wrench was in the Hall at 10:00 PM.

**Clue 4:**
- Sgt. Gray moved from the Cloak Room to the Gazebo at 03:00 AM.

**Clue 5:**
- Mr. Green or Wrench was in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM or Wrench was in the Hall at 10:00 PM (violates Clue 3, forcing our decision).

**Clue 6 (Branch 1 & Branch 2):**
- Sgt. Gray and Monsieur Brunette were in the Hall together at least once.
-测试继续，假设We continue with the first branch.

### Step 3: Hypothesis Generation

#### Hypothesis 1: Mr. Green is motivated by Hatred

**Assume Mr. Green was in the Cloak Room at 11:00 PM.**
- Mr. Green must be in the same room as Mr. Boddy at 12:00 AM.
- Assume Mr. Boddy was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM (since he was in the Hall at 10:00 PM).
- Mr. Green moves to the Cloak Room or remains there.

#### Clue 3:
- Mr. Green or Wrench was in the Hall at 10:00 PM (violates assumption, forcing us to consider the Wrench in the Hall).

#### Step 4: Branching

**Branch 1: Mr. Green in Cloak Room at 11:00 PM**
- *Scenario 1 (Fail Type B):* If Mr. Green is in Cloak Room at 11:00 PM, he cannot be in the same room as Mr. Boddy at 12:00 AM since we assume Mr. Boddy was in the Hall at 10:00 PM. This fails.

**Branch 2: Mr. Green in Hall at 10:00 PM**
- *Scenario 2 (Fail Type B):* Mr. Green moves to Hall at 10:00 PM, but he has to be in the same room as Mr. Boddy at 12:00 AM, which is a failure.

#### Hypothesis 2: Next Suspect

**Assume Sgt. Gray is motivated by Hatred**

**Clue 4:**
- Sgt. Gray moved from the Cloak Room to the Gazebo at 03:00 AM.

**Scenario 1:**
- Sgt. Gray was in Cloak Room at 02:00 AM.
- Mr. Boddy was in Cloak Room at 02:00 AM (Clue 9).

#### Clue 9:
- Mr. Boddy was in the Cloak Room at 02:00 AM if and only if Monsieur Brunette was in the Gazebo at 12:00 AM.

**Scenario 2 (Fail Type A):**
- Sgt. Gray and Monsieur Brunette being in the Hall together at least once (Clue 6) and moving to Gazebo at 12:00 AM violates choices.

#### Step 5: Continued Hypothesis

**Assume Mrs. Peacock is motivated by Hatred**

**Clue 12:**
- Mrs. Peacock moves from the Trophy Room to the Hall at 02:00 AM.

**Clue 16:**
- Mrs. Peacock was in the Trophy Room at 10:00 PM, but she is not in the same room with Mr. Boddy at 12:00 AM (Clue 12).

#### Step 6: Final Hypothesis Testing

**Assume Mr. Green is motivated by Greed**

**Clue 10:**
- Sgt. Gray and Monsieur Brunette are in the Hall at 11:00 PM.

**Clue 11:**
- Mr. Boddy was in the Billiard Room at 01:00 AM if and only if Monsieur Brunette was in the Gazebo at 01:00 AM.

#### Final Clue Analysis

**Clue 21:**
- Mr. Boddy was in the Cloak Room at 10:00 PM (Clue 10).

**Clue 22:**
- Mrs. White was in the Hall at 03:00 AM.

**Final Clue 25:**

### Step 3: Evaluate Biconditional Logic Gates

#### 01:00 AM:
- Mr. Boddy is in the Billiard Room if and only if the suspect motivated by Greed is in the Gazebo.
- The suspect motivated by Greed must be in the Gazebo at 01:00 AM (Clue 9).

#### 12:00 AM:
- The suspect motivated by Hatred must be in the same room as Mr. Boddy (Clue 1).
- The suspect motivated by Greed could be in the Billiard Room or Trophy Room at 12:00 AM (Clue 12).

### Step 4: Identify the Murderer

- Mr. Boddy was killed with the Revolver (Clue 28).
- The suspect motivated by Greed must have moved the Revolver to the Gazebo (Clue 25).
- At 12:00 AM, the suspect motivated by Greed is in the Billiard Room (Clue 29).

### Step 5: Determine the Murder

- The suspect motivated by Greed, Mrs. Peacock, and the Revolver are together at 12:00 AM (Clue 27).
- Mr. Boddy (dead) and the suspect motivated by Greed are in the Billiard Room at 12:00 AM (Clue 1).

### Step 6: Fill the Final Answers

**A. SUSPECT** - The suspect motivated by Greed.
**B. ROOM** - Billiard Room.
**C. TIME** - 12:00 AM.
**D. MOTIVE** - Greed.
**E. ROOM** - Cloak Room.
**F. ROOM** - Billiard Room.
**G. ROOM** - Trophy Room.

### Final Answers:
A. **Greed**
B. **Billiard Room**
C. **12:00 AM**
D. **Greed**
E. **Cloak Room**
F. **Billiard Room**
G. **Trophy Room**

### Step 1: Organize the Clues

**Names of Suspects:**
- Mr. Green
- Monsieur Brunette
- Mrs. White
- Sgt. Gray
- Mrs. Peacock

**Weapons:**
- Revolver
- Poison
- Wrench

**Possible Motives:**
- Revenge
- Pride
- Anger
- Greed
- Hatred

**Murderer's Motive:**
- The murderer used the Revolver.

**Murder Room:**
- One of the following: Billiard Room, Hall, Trophy Room, Cloak Room, Gazebo, Drawing Room.

**Murder Times:**
- 10:00 PM, 11:00 PM, 12:00 AM, 01:00 AM, 02:00 AM, 03:00 AM

### Step 2: Identify the Murderer, Room, and Time

We will start with the definitive clues:

- Mr. Boddy was in the Cloak Room at 10:00 PM.
- Mr. Boddy was in the Hall at 11:00 PM.
- Mr. Boddy was in the room just west of the Revolver at 11:00 PM.
- Mr. Boddy was in the same room as the Poison at 11:00 PM.
- Sgt. Gray was in the Billiard Room at 10:00 PM.
- Sgt. Gray moved from the Cloak Room to the Gazebo at 03:00 AM.
- Sgt. Gray and Monsieur Brunette were in the Hall together at least once.
- Sgt. Gray was in the Hall at 11:00 PM as well.
- The Revolver was in the Hall at 10:00 PM.
- The suspect motivated by Greed and the Poison were in the Billiard Room together at least once.
- Mr. Boddy was killed with the Revolver.

From these clues, we deduce:

- At 11:00 PM, Mr. Boddy was in the Hall and it was also in the same room as the Revolver and the Poison.
- The Poison suggests a motive of Greed.
- Hence, the suspect motivated by Greed was in the Hall at 11:00 PM.

Let's evaluate the movement possibilities to narrow it down:

### Hypothesis Generation and Testing

1. **Assume Sgt. Gray was the Greed-motivated suspect:**
   - Sgt. Gray was in the Billiard Room at 10:00 PM.
   - Sgt. Gray moved to the Gazebo at 03:00 AM.
   - Sgt. Gray and Mr. Boddy must have been in the Hall at 11:00 PM.
   - Therefore, Sgt. Gray must have moved from the Billiard Room to the Hall.

2. **Verification with room coordinates:**
   - Billiard Room: (0, 0)
   - Hall: (1, 0)
   - Gazebo: (1, 1)

   The distance between Billiard Room (0, 0) and Hall (1, 0) is 1, so the movement is allowed.

3. **Assume movements are correct:**
   - At 03:00 AM, Sgt. Gray moved to the Gazebo (1, 1).
   - Mr. Boddy was in the Hall (1, 0) at 11:00 PM and 12:00 AM.

4. **Evaluate the other suspects:**
   - Mr. Green was in the Cloak Room at 11:00 PM.
   - Monsieur Brunette was in the Hall at 11:00 PM.
   - Mrs. White was in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM or Hall at 03:00 AM.
   - Mrs. Peacock was in the Trophy Room at 10:00 PM and 11:00 PM.

Given that Sgt. Gray and Mr. Boddy were together in the Hall, this room satisfies the "alone in a room with the weapon" condition.

### Step 3: Confirm the Final Hypothesis

- **Murderer:** Sgt. Gray
- **Room:** Hall
- **Time:** 11:00 PM

### Step 4: Reveal the Motive

- The motive for the murder was Greed, as Sgt. Gray had the Poison and was responsible.

### Final Answers

A. Suspect: Sgt. Gray
B. Room: Hall
C. Time: 11:00 PM
D. Motive: Greed
